# Blue Archive contextual character coverage index

## 0. Responsibility and inheritance

This companion supplies the current supplemental additions to the [main coverage index](BLUE_ARCHIVE_CHARACTER_ANALYTICAL_COVERAGE_INDEX.md). The original index retains all 480-main evidence and sequential history. Its §§3–5 tables are the dated main-only snapshot. For the 262 subjects below, these five tables supersede the affected snapshot rows; the other 254 existing subject rows are inherited unchanged. Read the two indexes together, counting each subject once. Source-local role buckets remain analytical routes, not claims about distinct biographies.

**Current combined census: 23 PARTIAL_MODEL /493 UNMODELED /516 analytical subjects. All standalone models are NONE; no operational or bounded-validated model is admitted.** The98 existing main subjects gain contextual overrides;164 supplemental analytical additions remain UNMODELED, with254 main rows inherited unchanged. Cycle006 adds eight securely joined named people, one event-local ケイ actor with unresolved person/variant joins, and two EVENT80001 E133-local animal/voice buckets; four previously inherited main subjects now gain overrides. Serina, Sensei, baseball Rei, Niya and Love expand existing companion rows. Baseball Rei/CH0245 already existed; no duplicate row is created. The earlier153 additions and94 overrides remain intact. Local roles, animals, collectives, output devices and source costumes are analytical routes, not certified distinct human biographies. Kazusa and Reisa alone changed readiness in cycle001, from UNMODELED to distributed PARTIAL_MODEL for its familiar/care/conflict and written domains; [cycle001 §4](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/BLUE_ARCHIVE_PHASE2_CONTEXTUALIZATION_CYCLE_001_CHECKPOINT.md#4-readiness-adjudication) owns that basis. E017 remains counterevidence to lasting cessation or cure; these mechanisms do not transfer automatically to strangers, romance, institutional professionalism, general crises or main chronology.

Exactly213 supplemental objects are admitted with limits:65 group/59 event/37 bond/37 MomoTalk/15 character_data. [Cycle006](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/BLUE_ARCHIVE_PHASE2_CONTEXTUALIZATION_CYCLE_006_CHECKPOINT.md) accepts Serina's complete17-source private/written pool and all16 independent EVENT80001 encounters; all180 prior admissions remain intact. The [object crosswalk](BLUE_ARCHIVE_SUPPLEMENTAL_SOURCE_TO_ANALYSIS_CROSSWALK.csv) and [source-class crosswalk](BLUE_ARCHIVE_SOURCE_CLASS_CROSSWALK.md) own exact IDs, witnesses, admission and exclusions. Other supplemental objects remain unadmitted even where full draft readings or review receipts exist. Package inspection does not establish every subject's appearance. Eimi's printed speaker is secure where named but its empty person join remains unresolved; Nel/Neru is one source identity, Chihiro is CH0160, Natsu CH0155 and Reisa CH0167. Reijo is separate from the earlier unnamed Shanhaijing chief; generic roles are not merged across encounters. Nozomi/CH0243 is distinct from Nonomi; Subaru's raw 니코 스바루 is not FOX Niko. Event-local ケイ keeps absent person/variant joins and candidateCH0335/CHAR_101350001 without a main Key/Kei.sav merger. Crow/black-cat roles are episode-local, without a human biography or cross-event identity.

Coverage and readiness have separate meanings. The inherited H/M/L/GAP/SPECIAL vocabulary applies. Accepted ordinary pleasure, taste, care, humor, labor and friction can earn meaningful situated coverage without raising model readiness. Source classes, chronology, attribution, branch alternatives, private audience and performed voice retain their recorded limits. No new main-state transition is inferred from a release date, episode number or packet order. Phase 2 and every full arc acceptance row remain IN_PROGRESS.

## 6. Cycle 001 coverage delta — 2026-10-01

The accepted readings add ordinary and peer contexts across Abydos, C&C, Veritas, the Sweets/Vigilante encounters, and nine independent gift visits. Ayane's bought refreshments and strictness, C&C's cleaning and styling pleasure, Yuuka's fairness despite temptation, Kazusa's café aspiration, Reisa's attachment, and distinct gift-making practices are positive literary evidence. They retain intrinsic analytical value without a high-stakes test.

Only Kazusa and Reisa meet the event-local conditional-mechanism threshold in this tranche. Every other existing readiness state is inherited, and every new subject remains UNMODELED. Group hospitality does not fill a complete private baseline, engineering pleasure does not repair Rio's public accountability, and partial ethics/security contrasts do not prove institutional competence. The source-facing readings retain counterreadings and attribution exclusions. No standalone model, forecast freeze, prediction register, or prospective test result is created here.

Future admitted cycles update this companion and its dated deltas, preserving the main index's history. The [whole-phase audit](../08%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/BLUE_ARCHIVE_PHASE2_CONTEXTUALIZATION_AUDIT.md) remains the completion authority. An accepted packet and a usable local mechanism do not certify a completed arc or Phase 3 package.
